      END, [u0].[Id], [u0].[full_name], [u0].[email], [u0].[phone], [u0].[city], [u0].[fiqh], CASE
          WHEN [s0].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
          ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
      END, [s0].[Id], [s0].[name], [s0].[duration_minutes], [s0].[price], [a1].[Id], [a1].[service_request_id], [a1].[scheduled_at], [a1].[c], [a1].[c0], [a1].[duration_minutes], [a1].[status], [a1].[alim_feedback_skipped], [a1].[c1], [a1].[c2], [a1].[c3], [a1].[c4], [a1].[c5], [a1].[c6], [r6].[rating], [r6].[comment], [r6].[createdAt], [r6].[c], [r8].[rating], [r8].[comment], [r8].[createdAt], [r8].[c]
      FROM [dbo].[service_requests] AS [s]
      INNER JOIN [dbo].[users] AS [u] ON [s].[customer_id] = [u].[Id]
      LEFT JOIN [dbo].[users] AS [u0] ON [s].[alim_id] = [u0].[Id]
      LEFT JOIN [dbo].[services] AS [s0] ON [s].[service_id] = [s0].[Id]
      OUTER APPLY (
          SELECT TOP(1) [a0].[Id], [a0].[service_request_id], [a0].[scheduled_at], CASE
              WHEN [a0].[duration_minutes] IS NOT NULL THEN DATEADD(minute, CAST(CAST([a0].[duration_minutes] AS float) AS int), [a0].[scheduled_at])
          END AS [c], CASE
              WHEN [a0].[status] = N'completed' THEN [a0].[updated_at]
          END AS [c0], [a0].[duration_minutes], [a0].[status], [a0].[alim_feedback_skipped], CASE
              WHEN [a0].[status] = N'completed' THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
              ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
          END AS [c1], CASE
              WHEN [a0].[status] = N'completed' AND NOT EXISTS (
                  SELECT 1
                  FROM [dbo].[reviews] AS [r]
                  WHERE [r].[appointment_id] = [a0].[Id] AND [r].[reviewer_id] = [s].[customer_id] AND [r].[reviewee_id] = [s].[alim_id]) THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
              ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
          END AS [c2], CASE
              WHEN [a0].[status] = N'completed' AND NOT EXISTS (
                  SELECT 1
                  FROM [dbo].[reviews] AS [r0]
                  WHERE [r0].[appointment_id] = [a0].[Id] AND ([r0].[reviewer_id] = [s].[alim_id] OR ([r0].[reviewer_id] IS NULL AND [s].[alim_id] IS NULL)) AND [r0].[reviewee_id] = [s].[customer_id]) THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
              ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
          END AS [c3], CASE
              WHEN EXISTS (

                  FROM [dbo].[reviews] AS [r1]
                  WHERE [r1].[appointment_id] = [a0].[Id] AND [r1].[reviewer_id] = [s].[customer_id] AND [r1].[reviewee_id] = [s].[alim_id]) THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
              ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
          END AS [c4], CASE
              WHEN EXISTS (
                  SELECT 1
                  FROM [dbo].[reviews] AS [r2]
                  WHERE [r2].[appointment_id] = [a0].[Id] AND ([r2].[reviewer_id] = [s].[alim_id] OR ([r2].[reviewer_id] IS NULL AND [s].[alim_id] IS NULL)) AND [r2].[reviewee_id] = [s].[customer_id]) THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
              ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
          END AS [c5], 1 AS [c6]
          FROM [dbo].[appointments] AS [a0]
          WHERE [a0].[service_request_id] = [s].[Id]
          ORDER BY [a0].[created_at] DESC
      ) AS [a1]

          SELECT [r5].[rating], [r5].[comment], [r5].[createdAt], [r5].[c], [r5].[appointment_id], [r5].[reviewer_id], [r5].[reviewee_id]

              SELECT [r3].[rating], [r3].[comment], [r3].[created_at] AS [createdAt], 1 AS [c], [r3].[appointment_id], [r3].[reviewer_id], [r3].[reviewee_id], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [r3].[appointment_id], [r3].[reviewer_id], [r3].[reviewee_id] ORDER BY [r3].[created_at] DESC) AS [row]
              FROM [dbo].[reviews] AS [r3]
          ) AS [r5]
          WHERE [r5].[row] <= 1
      ) AS [r6] ON [a1].[Id] = [r6].[appointment_id] AND ([s].[alim_id] = [r6].[reviewer_id] OR ([s].[alim_id] IS NULL AND [r6].[reviewer_id] IS NULL)) AND [s].[customer_id] = [r6].[reviewee_id]

              FROM [dbo].[reviews] AS [r4]
          ) AS [r7]
          WHERE [r7].[row] <= 1
      ) AS [r8] ON [a1].[Id] = [r8].[appointment_id] AND [s].[customer_id] = [r8].[reviewer_id] AND [s].[alim_id] = [r8].[reviewee_id]

      ORDER BY [s].[created_at] DESC

      FROM [dbo].[favorite_alims] AS [f]
